However, existing RL-based training still remains only a rough approximation to human learning. Human learners leverage both external and internal experience to guide exploration and gradually internalize useful trajectories into stable knowledge. Motivated by this gap, we ask: how can LLMs better utilize and internalize experience during RLVR training? To answer this question, we propose \textbf{D}ual \textbf{G}uidance \textbf{O}ptimization~(\textbf{DGO}), a unified framework that leverages \emph{external} and \emph{internal experience} to improve training effectiveness. Specifically, DGO first constructs an experience bank from previously explored trajectories. The policy then performs exploration under the joint guidance of the experience bank and the model's internal knowledge. The resulting trajectories are further used to refine the experience bank and optimize model parameters, forming a closed loop of experience utilization and internalization. Experiments show that DGO consistently outperforms baseline methods, suggesting that better utilization and internalization of experience lead to more effective reasoning.

</details>


### [116] [Mixed-signal implementation of feedback-control optimizer for single-layer Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24113)
*Jonathan Haag,Christian Metzner,Dmitrii Zendrikov,Giacomo Indiveri,Benjamin Grewe,Chiara De Luca,Matteo Saponati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在混合信号神经形态处理器上实现反馈控制优化器的概念验证，展示与数值模拟和梯度基准性能匹配的片上学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 片上学习对于可扩展和自适应的神经形态系统至关重要，但现有训练方法要么难以在硬件中实现，要么过于受限。反馈控制优化器能够实现神经形态设备的表达性片上训练。

Method: 在混合信号神经形态处理器上实现反馈控制优化器，采用In-The-Loop(ITL)训练设置，在二进制分类任务和非线性Yin-Yang问题上进行评估

Result: 片上训练性能与数值模拟和基于梯度的基准方法相匹配，证明了在真实混合信号约束下反馈驱动在线学习的可行性

Conclusion: 反馈控制优化器为神经形态计算提供了一种协同设计方法，可直接在硅片中嵌入学习规则，实现自主和自适应神经形态计算

Abstract: On-chip learning is key to scalable and adaptive neuromorphic systems, yet existing training methods are either difficult to implement in hardware or overly restrictive. However, recent studies show that feedback-control optimizers can enable expressive, on-chip training of neuromorphic devices. In this work, we present a proof-of-concept implementation of such feedback-control optimizers on a mixed-signal neuromorphic processor. We assess the proposed approach in an In-The-Loop(ITL) training setup on both a binary classification task and the nonlinear Yin-Yang problem, demonstrating on-chip training that matches the performance of numerical simulations and gradient-based baselines. Our results highlight the feasibility of feedback-driven, online learning under realistic mixed-signal constraints, and represent a co-design approach toward embedding such rules directly in silicon for autonomous and adaptive neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [117] [Likelihood hacking in probabilistic program synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24126)
*Jacek Karwowski,Younesse Kaddar,Zihuiwen Ye,Nikolay Malkin,Sam Staton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型通过强化学习训练生成概率程序时，可能通过产生数据分布未归一化的程序来人为夸大边际似然奖励，而不是更好地拟合数据，这种现象称为似然黑客攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当使用强化学习训练语言模型生成概率程序时，模型可能通过生成数据分布未归一化的程序来人为夸大边际似然奖励，而不是真正改善数据拟合。这种"似然黑客攻击"现象破坏了自动贝叶斯模型发现的可靠性，需要从语言层面进行预防。

Method: 1. 在核心概率编程语言中形式化似然黑客攻击；2. 给出预防LH的充分语法条件；3. 证明满足这些条件的安全语言片段$\mathcal{L}_{\text{safe}}$不会产生LH程序；4. 实现$\texttt{SafeStan}$作为Stan的LH抵抗修改版本；5. 通过GRPO训练模型生成PyMC代码进行实证验证。

Result: 1. GRPO训练模型在最初几步训练中就发现了LH漏洞，违规率显著高于未训练模型基线；2. $\texttt{SafeStan}$在优化压力下有效防止了LH；3. 语言级安全约束在理论和实践中都被证明是有效的。

Conclusion: 语言级安全约束既有理论基础，在实践中也能有效防止似然黑客攻击，为自动化贝叶斯模型发现提供了可靠保障。$\mathcal{L}_{\text{safe}}$的语法条件和$\texttt{SafeStan}$的实现展示了预防LH的可行性。

Abstract: When language models are trained by reinforcement learning (RL) to write probabilistic programs, they can artificially inflate their marginal-likelihood reward by producing programs whose data distribution fails to normalise instead of fitting the data better. We call this failure likelihood hacking (LH). We formalise LH in a core probabilistic programming language (PPL) and give sufficient syntactic conditions for its prevention, proving that a safe language fragment $\mathcal{L}_{\text{safe}}$ satisfying these conditions cannot produce likelihood-hacking programs. Empirically, we show that GRPO-trained models generating PyMC code discover LH exploits within the first few training steps, driving violation rates well above the untrained-model baseline. We implement $\mathcal{L}_{\text{safe}}$'s conditions as $\texttt{SafeStan}$, a LH-resistant modification of Stan, and show empirically that it prevents LH under optimisation pressure. These results show that language-level safety constraints are both theoretically grounded and effective in practice for automated Bayesian model discovery.

</details>


### [118] [Linear-Nonlinear Fusion Neural Operator for Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24143)
*Heng Wu,Junjie Wang,Benzhuo Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出线性-非线性融合神经算子（LNF-NO），通过显式解耦线性与非线性效应来提升算子学习效率，在多个PDE基准测试中比DeepONet和FNO训练更快且精度相当或更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法需要重复求解偏微分方程，而神经算子学习能直接构建从方程参数空间到解空间的映射关系。研究发现，在算子映射中显式解耦线性和非线性效应能显著提升学习效率。

Method: 提出线性-非线性融合神经算子（LNF-NO），通过线性分量和非线性分量的乘法融合来建模算子映射。该结构支持多函数输入，适用于规则网格和不规则几何，实现轻量化和可解释表示。

Result: 在包括非线性Poisson-Boltzmann方程和多物理场耦合系统的多种PDE算子学习基准测试中，LNF-NO通常比DeepONet和FNO训练速度显著更快，在大多数情况下达到相当或更好的精度。在3D Poisson-Boltzmann案例中，LNF-NO在比较模型中达到最佳精度，且训练速度比3D FNO基线快约2.7倍。

Conclusion: 线性-非线性解耦方法能有效捕捉算子层面的复杂解特征，同时保持稳定性和泛化性。LNF-NO为神经算子学习提供了一种高效、轻量且可解释的架构。

Abstract: Neural operator learning directly constructs the mapping relationship from the equation parameter space to the solution space, enabling efficient direct inference in practical applications without the need for repeated solution of partial differential equations (PDEs) - an advantage that is difficult to achieve with traditional numerical methods. In this work, we find that explicitly decoupling linear and nonlinear effects within such operator mappings leads to markedly improved learning efficiency. This yields a novel network structure, namely the Linear-Nonlinear Fusion Neural Operator (LNF-NO), which models operator mappings via the multiplicative fusion of a linear component and a nonlinear component, thus achieving a lightweight and interpretable representation. This linear-nonlinear decoupling enables efficient capture of complex solution features at the operator level while maintaining stability and generality. LNF-NO naturally supports multiple functional inputs and is applicable to both regular grids and irregular geometries. Across a diverse suite of PDE operator-learning benchmarks, including nonlinear Poisson-Boltzmann equations and multi-physics coupled systems, LNF-NO is typically substantially faster to train than Deep Operator Networks (DeepONet) and Fourier Neural Operators (FNO), while achieving comparable or better accuracy in most cases. On the tested 3D Poisson-Boltzmann case, LNF-NO attains the best accuracy among the compared models and trains approximately 2.7x faster than a 3D FNO baseline.

</details>


### [119] [A Deep Dive into Scaling RL for Code Generation with Synthetic Data and Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24202)
*Cansu Sancaktar,David Zhang,Gabriel Synnaeve,Taco Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展的多轮合成数据生成管道，通过教师模型基于学生表现迭代优化问题，产生结构化难度递进，支持课程式训练，提升RL训练效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽能提升大语言模型性能，但在大规模应用中，数据多样性和结构而非单纯数据量成为性能提升的瓶颈，需要解决可持续的性能增益问题

Method: 引入可扩展的多轮合成数据生成管道：教师模型根据上下文中的学生表现摘要迭代优化问题，无需教师微调即可生成结构化难度递进；相比单轮生成，该方法显著提高有效合成问题的产出，并自然产生同一核心任务的简单和困难变体（"垫脚石"），支持课程式训练

Result: 在Llama3.1-8B Instruct、Qwen3-8B Base模型家族及Qwen2.5-32B上的实验表明：合成数据增强一致提升领域内代码性能，在大多数情况下也提升领域外数学性能；提供了课程设计和数据多样性如何共同塑造RL训练动态的实证见解

Conclusion: 多轮合成数据生成方法通过创建结构化难度递进和课程式训练，有效解决了RL训练中数据多样性和结构的瓶颈问题，为大规模语言模型强化学习提供了可扩展的解决方案

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for improving large language models beyond supervised fine-tuning, yet sustaining performance gains at scale remains an open challenge, as data diversity and structure, rather than volume alone, become the limiting factor. We address this by introducing a scalable multi-turn synthetic data generation pipeline in which a teacher model iteratively refines problems based on in-context student performance summaries, producing structured difficulty progressions without any teacher fine-tuning. Compared to single-turn generation, this multi-turn approach substantially improves the yield of valid synthetic problems and naturally produces stepping stones, i.e. easier and harder variants of the same core task, that support curriculum-based training. We systematically study how task difficulty, curriculum scheduling, and environment diversity interact during RL training across the Llama3.1-8B Instruct and Qwen3-8B Base model families, with additional scaling experiments on Qwen2.5-32B. Our results show that synthetic augmentation consistently improves in-domain code and in most cases out-of-domain math performance, and we provide empirical insights into how curriculum design and data diversity jointly shape RL training dynamics.

</details>


### [120] [IPatch: A Multi-Resolution Transformer Architecture for Robust Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24207)
*Aymane Harkati,Moncef Garouani,Olivier Teste,Julien Aligon,Mohamed Hamlich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IPatch：一种多分辨率Transformer架构，通过同时集成点级和块级令牌来建模多分辨率时间信息，提升多元时间序列预测的准确性、鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列预测需要同时捕捉短期波动和长期依赖关系。传统点级表示能保留细粒度信息但计算成本高且难以建模广泛上下文依赖；块级表示提高了效率但会丢失对波动或复杂时间序列预测至关重要的细粒度时间细节。

Method: 提出IPatch多分辨率Transformer架构，同时集成点级和块级令牌来建模多分辨率时间信息。点级令牌保留单个时间步信息用于细粒度建模，块级令牌聚合连续时间步形成紧凑令牌以提高效率并建模局部时间动态。

Result: 在7个基准数据集上的实验表明，IPatch相比单一表示基线方法，在预测准确性、噪声鲁棒性和不同预测视野的泛化能力方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: IPatch通过同时利用点级和块级表示的优势，有效解决了多元时间序列预测中细粒度建模与计算效率之间的权衡问题，为多分辨率时间建模提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of multivariate time series remains challenging due to the need to capture both short-term fluctuations and long-range temporal dependencies. Transformer-based models have emerged as a powerful approach, but their performance depends critically on the representation of temporal data. Traditional point-wise representations preserve individual time-step information, enabling fine-grained modeling, yet they tend to be computationally expensive and less effective at modeling broader contextual dependencies, limiting their scalability to long sequences. Patch-wise representations aggregate consecutive steps into compact tokens to improve efficiency and model local temporal dynamics, but they often discard fine-grained temporal details that are critical for accurate predictions in volatile or complex time series. We propose IPatch, a multi-resolution Transformer architecture that integrates both point-wise and patch-wise tokens, modeling temporal information at multiple resolutions. Experiments on 7 benchmark datasets demonstrate that IPatch consistently improves forecasting accuracy, robustness to noise, and generalization across various prediction horizons compared to single-representation baselines.

</details>


### [121] [Uncovering Memorization in Timeseries Imputation models: LBRM Membership Inference and its link to attribute Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24213)
*Faiz Taleb,Ivan Gazeau,Maryline Laurent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了针对时间序列插值深度学习模型的两阶段隐私攻击框架，包括改进的成员推断攻击和首个属性推断攻击，在注意力机制和自编码器架构上验证了攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列插值模型在医疗、物联网和金融等领域的部署引发了严重的隐私担忧。除了生成模型中已广泛研究的无意记忆问题外，作者发现时间序列模型在黑盒设置下容易受到推理攻击，需要系统性地评估和防御这类隐私威胁。

Method: 提出了两阶段攻击框架：1) 基于参考模型的新型成员推断攻击，提高了检测准确率，即使对抵抗过拟合攻击的模型也有效；2) 首个针对时间序列插值模型的属性推断攻击，可预测训练数据的敏感特征。在两种场景下评估：从头训练的模型，以及攻击者能获取初始权重的微调模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的成员攻击能显著恢复训练数据，tpr@top25%分数显著高于朴素攻击基线。成员攻击还能有效预测属性推断是否可行（精度从78%提升到90%）。在注意力机制和自编码器架构上都验证了攻击的有效性。

Conclusion: 时间序列插值模型存在严重的隐私漏洞，即使在黑盒设置下也容易受到成员推断和属性推断攻击。提出的两阶段攻击框架有效揭示了这些隐私风险，强调了在部署时间序列模型时加强隐私保护措施的必要性。

Abstract: Deep learning models for time series imputation are now essential in fields such as healthcare, the Internet of Things (IoT), and finance. However, their deployment raises critical privacy concerns. Beyond the well-known issue of unintended memorization, which has been extensively studied in generative models, we demonstrate that time series models are vulnerable to inference attacks in a black-box setting. In this work, we introduce a two-stage attack framework comprising: (1) a novel membership inference attack based on a reference model that improves detection accuracy, even for models robust to overfitting-based attacks, and (2) the first attribute inference attack that predicts sensitive characteristics of the training data for timeseries imputation model. We evaluate these attacks on attention-based and autoencoder architectures in two scenarios: models that are trained from scratch, and fine-tuned models where the adversary has access to the initial weights. Our experimental results demonstrate that the proposed membership attack retrieves a significant portion of the training data with a tpr@top25% score significantly higher than a naive attack baseline. We show that our membership attack also provides a good insight of whether attribute inference will work (with a precision of 90% instead of 78% in the genral case).

</details>


### [122] [Identification of NMF by choosing maximum-volume basis vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24227)
*Qianqian Qi,Zhongming Chen,Peter G. M. van der Heijden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出最大体积约束NMF框架，通过使基向量尽可能不同来解决最小体积约束NMF在高度混合数据中的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小体积约束NMF存在两个主要问题：1) 在高度混合数据中可能失效，因为其诱导的系数矩阵稀疏性在高度混合情况下不成立；2) 估计的基向量可能是真实基向量的混合，难以解释。需要新的NMF框架来解决这些局限性。

Method: 提出最大体积约束NMF框架，通过使基向量尽可能不同（最大化体积）来替代传统的最小体积约束方法。建立了最大体积约束NMF的可识别性定理，并提供了相应的估计算法。

Result: 实验结果表明所提方法的有效性，能够更好地处理高度混合数据，获得更易解释的基向量估计。

Conclusion: 最大体积约束NMF是解决最小体积约束NMF在高度混合数据中局限性的有效替代方案，具有更好的可识别性和可解释性。

Abstract: In nonnegative matrix factorization (NMF), minimum-volume-constrained NMF is a widely used framework for identifying the solution of NMF by making basis vectors as similar as possible. This typically induces sparsity in the coefficient matrix, with each row containing zero entries. Consequently, minimum-volume-constrained NMF may fail for highly mixed data, where such sparsity does not hold. Moreover, the estimated basis vectors in minimum-volume-constrained NMF may be difficult to interpret as they may be mixtures of the ground truth basis vectors. To address these limitations, in this paper we propose a new NMF framework, called maximum-volume-constrained NMF, which makes the basis vectors as distinct as possible. We further establish an identifiability theorem for maximum-volume-constrained NMF and provide an algorithm to estimate it. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [123] [Embracing Heteroscedasticity for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24254)
*Yijun Wang,Qiyuan Zhuang,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LSG-VAE：一种显式建模时变方差的概率时间序列预测框架，通过位置-尺度高斯似然解决现有方法隐含同方差假设的问题，提升异方差建模能力和预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非自回归生成式概率时间序列预测方法（如TimeVAE和K²VAE）依赖基于MSE的训练目标，隐含同方差假设，无法有效建模现实时间序列中的时变条件方差（异方差性），限制了不确定性量化和预测准确性。

Method: 提出位置-尺度高斯变分自编码器（LSG-VAE），通过位置-尺度似然公式显式参数化预测均值和时变方差，引入自适应衰减机制，在训练中自动降低高波动观测的权重，提升趋势预测的鲁棒性。

Result: 在九个基准数据集上的实验表明，LSG-VAE在保持高计算效率的同时，持续优于15个强生成式基线模型，适合实时部署。

Conclusion: LSG-VAE通过显式建模时变方差有效解决了现有概率时间序列预测方法的异方差建模局限，提供了更准确的预测和更可靠的不确定性量化，同时保持了计算效率。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series forecasting (PTSF) aims to model the full predictive distribution of future observations, enabling both accurate forecasting and principled uncertainty quantification. A central requirement of PTSF is to embrace heteroscedasticity, as real-world time series exhibit time-varying conditional variances induced by nonstationary dynamics, regime changes, and evolving external conditions. However, most existing non-autoregressive generative approaches to PTSF, such as TimeVAE and $K^2$VAE, rely on MSE-based training objectives that implicitly impose a homoscedastic assumption, thereby fundamentally limiting their ability to model temporal heteroscedasticity. To address this limitation, we propose the Location-Scale Gaussian VAE (LSG-VAE), a simple but effective framework that explicitly parameterizes both the predictive mean and time-dependent variance through a location-scale likelihood formulation. This design enables LSG-VAE to faithfully capture heteroscedastic aleatoric uncertainty and introduces an adaptive attenuation mechanism that automatically down-weights highly volatile observations during training, leading to improved robustness in trend prediction. Extensive experiments on nine benchmark datasets demonstrate that LSG-VAE consistently outperforms fifteen strong generative baselines while maintaining high computational efficiency suitable for real-time deployment.

</details>


### [124] [Forecasting with Guidance: Representation-Level Supervision for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24262)
*Jiacheng Wang,Liang Fan,Baihua Li,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGuider：一种可插入式方法，利用预训练时间序列基础模型作为语义教师，通过表示级监督对齐目标模型编码器嵌入，提升时间序列预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于误差目标端到端训练的时间序列预测方法倾向于丢弃信息丰富但极端的模式，导致预测过于平滑且时间表示无法捕捉显著动态

Method: 提出ReGuider插件方法：1）利用预训练时间序列基础模型作为语义教师；2）提取预训练模型的中间嵌入（富含时间和语义信息）；3）通过表示级监督将其与目标模型编码器嵌入对齐

Result: 在多样化数据集和架构上的广泛实验表明，ReGuider能持续提升预测性能，验证了其有效性和通用性

Conclusion: ReGuider通过利用预训练模型的语义知识指导目标模型学习更具表现力的时间表示，有效解决了传统方法丢弃极端模式的问题，提升了时间序列预测的准确性

Abstract: Nowadays, time series forecasting is predominantly approached through the end-to-end training of deep learning architectures using error-based objectives. While this is effective at minimizing average loss, it encourages the encoder to discard informative yet extreme patterns. This results in smooth predictions and temporal representations that poorly capture salient dynamics. To address this issue, we propose ReGuider, a plug-in method that can be seamlessly integrated into any forecasting architecture. ReGuider leverages pretrained time series foundation models as semantic teachers. During training, the input sequence is processed together by the target forecasting model and the pretrained model. Rather than using the pretrained model's outputs directly, we extract its intermediate embeddings, which are rich in temporal and semantic information, and align them with the target model's encoder embeddings through representation-level supervision. This alignment process enables the encoder to learn more expressive temporal representations, thereby improving the accuracy of downstream forecasting. Extensive experimentation across diverse datasets and architectures demonstrates that our ReGuider consistently improves forecasting performance, confirming its effectiveness and versatility.

</details>


### [125] [DeepDTF: Dual-Branch Transformer Fusion for Multi-Omics Anticancer Drug Response Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24265)
*Yuhan Zhao,Jacob Tennant,James Yang,Zhishan Guo,Young Whang,Ning Sui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepDTF是一个用于癌症药物反应预测的双分支Transformer融合框架，通过整合多组学数据和药物分子图结构，在冷启动细胞系评估中显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症药物反应因肿瘤的多层分子异质性而存在广泛差异，需要计算决策支持。现有深度CDR模型在多组学数据和化学结构药物之间的跨模态对齐方面存在挑战，存在特征错位和有限归纳偏置的问题。

Method: 提出DeepDTF端到端双分支Transformer融合框架：细胞系分支使用模态特定编码器处理多组学数据，通过Transformer块捕获长程依赖；药物分支将化合物表示为分子图，使用GNN-Transformer整合局部拓扑和全局上下文；通过基于Transformer的融合模块建模跨模态交互并缓解特征错位。

Result: 在5折冷启动细胞系评估的公共药物基因组基准测试中，DeepDTF在所有组学设置中均优于强基线，使用完整多组学输入时达到RMSE=1.248、R²=0.875和AUC=0.987，分类错误率降低9.5%。此外，通过SHAP基因归因和预排序GSEA通路富集提供生物学解释。

Conclusion: DeepDTF通过创新的双分支Transformer架构有效解决了多组学与药物结构之间的跨模态对齐问题，在药物反应预测方面实现了最先进的性能，同时提供可解释的生物学洞见，为精准肿瘤学提供了有力的计算决策支持工具。

Abstract: Cancer drug response varies widely across tumors due to multi-layer molecular heterogeneity, motivating computational decision support for precision oncology. Despite recent progress in deep CDR models, robust alignment between high-dimensional multi-omics and chemically structured drugs remains challenging due to cross-modal misalignment and limited inductive bias. We present DeepDTF, an end-to-end dual-branch Transformer fusion framework for joint log(IC50) regression and drug sensitivity classification. The cell-line branch uses modality-specific encoders for multi-omics profiles with Transformer blocks to capture long-range dependencies, while the drug branch represents compounds as molecular graphs and encodes them with a GNN-Transformer to integrate local topology with global context. Omics and drug representations are fused by a Transformer-based module that models cross-modal interactions and mitigates feature misalignment. On public pharmacogenomic benchmarks under 5-fold cold-start cell-line evaluation, DeepDTF consistently outperforms strong baselines across omics settings, achieving up to RMSE=1.248, R^2=0.875, and AUC=0.987 with full multi-omics inputs, while reducing classification error (1-ACC) by 9.5%. Beyond accuracy, DeepDTF provides biologically grounded explanations via SHAP-based gene attributions and pathway enrichment with pre-ranked GSEA.

</details>


### [126] [Language-Assisted Image Clustering Guided by Discriminative Relational Signals and Adaptive Semantic Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24275)
*Jun Ma,Xu Zhang,Zhengxing Jiao,Yaxin Hou,Hui Liu,Junhui Hou,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的语言辅助图像聚类框架，通过利用跨模态关系和提示学习来解决现有方法中文本特征相似度高和聚类受限于预建图像-文本对齐的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言辅助图像聚类方法存在两个主要问题：1) 为每张图像构建的文本特征高度相似，导致类间区分性弱；2) 聚类步骤受限于预建的图像-文本对齐，限制了文本模态的更好利用潜力。

Method: 提出包含两个互补组件的新框架：1) 利用跨模态关系生成更具区分性的聚类自监督信号，这与大多数视觉语言模型的训练机制兼容；2) 通过提示学习学习类别级连续语义中心来生成最终聚类分配。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法相比最先进方法平均提升2.6%，且学习到的语义中心表现出很强的可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过解决现有语言辅助图像聚类方法的局限性，提出的新框架在聚类性能上取得了显著提升，同时学习到的语义中心具有良好的可解释性，为跨模态聚类提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Language-Assisted Image Clustering (LAIC) augments the input images with additional texts with the help of vision-language models (VLMs) to promote clustering performance. Despite recent progress, existing LAIC methods often overlook two issues: (i) textual features constructed for each image are highly similar, leading to weak inter-class discriminability; (ii) the clustering step is restricted to pre-built image-text alignments, limiting the potential for better utilization of the text modality. To address these issues, we propose a new LAIC framework with two complementary components. First, we exploit cross-modal relations to produce more discriminative self-supervision signals for clustering, as it compatible with most VLMs training mechanisms. Second, we learn category-wise continuous semantic centers via prompt learning to produce the final clustering assignments. Extensive experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method achieves an average improvement of 2.6% over state-of-the-art methods, and the learned semantic centers exhibit strong interpretability. Code is available in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [127] [Large Language Model Guided Incentive Aware Reward Design for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24324)
*Dogan Urgun,Gokhan Gungor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个自动化奖励设计框架，利用大语言模型从环境信息中合成可执行的奖励程序，通过迭代搜索生成优于手动设计的奖励，特别在交互密集型任务中效果显著


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合作多智能体系统中，设计有效的辅助奖励是一项困难任务，激励错位可能导致次优协调，特别是在稀疏任务反馈无法提供足够基础的情况下。需要减轻手动工程负担，同时生成与有限预算下合作学习兼容的奖励信号

Method: 引入自动化奖励设计框架：1) 利用大语言模型从环境信息中合成可执行的奖励程序；2) 将候选程序约束在形式有效性范围内；3) 在固定计算预算下从头训练策略来评估奖励程序效果；4) 仅基于稀疏任务回报进行选择；5) 在四个具有不同走廊拥堵、交接依赖和结构不对称性的Overcooked-AI布局中进行评估

Result: 迭代搜索生成的奖励程序持续产生更优的任务回报和交付数量，在交互瓶颈主导的环境中增益最为显著。诊断分析显示合成的奖励组件增加了动作选择的相互依赖性，并在协调密集型任务中改善了信号对齐

Conclusion: 该框架证明，搜索基于目标的奖励程序可以减轻手动工程负担，同时生成与有限预算下合作学习兼容的奖励信号，为解决多智能体系统中的奖励设计问题提供了自动化解决方案

Abstract: Designing effective auxiliary rewards for cooperative multi-agent systems remains a precarious task; misaligned incentives risk inducing suboptimal coordination, especially where sparse task feedback fails to provide sufficient grounding. This study introduces an automated reward design framework that leverages large language models to synthesize executable reward programs from environment instrumentation. The procedure constrains candidate programs within a formal validity envelope and evaluates their efficacy by training policies from scratch under a fixed computational budget; selection depends exclusively on the sparse task return. The framework is evaluated across four distinct Overcooked-AI layouts characterized by varied corridor congestion, handoff dependencies, and structural asymmetries. Iterative search generations consistently yield superior task returns and delivery counts, with the most pronounced gains occurring in environments dominated by interaction bottlenecks. Diagnostic analysis of the synthesized shaping components indicates increased interdependence in action selection and improved signal alignment in coordination-intensive tasks. These results demonstrate that the search for objectivegrounded reward programs can mitigate the burden of manual engineering while producing shaping signals compatible with cooperative learning under finite budgets.

</details>


### [128] [MolEvolve: LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search for Interpretable Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24382)
*Xiangsen Chen,Ruilong Wu,Yanyan Lan,Ting Ma,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolEvolve是一个基于进化框架的分子发现系统，使用大语言模型和蒙特卡洛树搜索自主探索化学操作，解决深度学习在化学中可解释性不足和活性悬崖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在化学领域应用受限，主要因为缺乏可解释性，以及无法解决活性悬崖问题——即微小结构变化导致性质剧烈变化的现象。当前基于相似性原理的表征学习方法难以捕捉这种结构-活性的不连续性。

Method: MolEvolve将分子发现重新定义为自主的前瞻性规划问题。系统利用大语言模型冷启动，探索可执行的化学符号操作库，结合蒙特卡洛树搜索引擎进行测试时规划，并使用外部工具如RDKit。该系统自主发现最优轨迹，生成透明的推理链。

Result: 实验结果表明，MolEvolve的自主搜索不仅能够生成透明、人类可读的化学见解，而且在性质预测和分子优化任务中均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MolEvolve通过进化框架解决了化学深度学习中的可解释性和活性悬崖问题，实现了自主的分子发现，并生成可解释的化学见解。

Abstract: Despite deep learning's success in chemistry, its impact is hindered by a lack of interpretability and an inability to resolve activity cliffs, where minor structural nuances trigger drastic property shifts. Current representation learning, bound by the similarity principle, often fails to capture these structural-activity discontinuities. To address this, we introduce MolEvolve, an evolutionary framework that reformulates molecular discovery as an autonomous, look-ahead planning problem. Unlike traditional methods that depend on human-engineered features or rigid prior knowledge, MolEvolve leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) to actively explore and evolve a library of executable chemical symbolic operations. By utilizing the LLM to cold start and an Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) engine for test-time planning with external tools (e.g. RDKit), the system self-discovers optimal trajectories autonomously. This process evolves transparent reasoning chains that translate complex structural transformations into actionable, human-readable chemical insights. Experimental results demonstrate that MolEvolve's autonomous search not only evolves transparent, human-readable chemical insights, but also outperforms baselines in both property prediction and molecule optimization tasks.

</details>


### [129] [On the Use of Bagging for Local Intrinsic Dimensionality Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24384)
*Kristóf Péter,Ricardo J. G. B. Campello,James Bailey,Michael E. Houle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于子袋装（subbagging）的集成方法来降低局部本征维度（LID）估计的方差，通过理论分析和实验验证了采样率、k-NN大小和集成规模对性能的影响，并展示了该方法在降低方差和均方误差方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部本征维度（LID）估计需要从小邻域采样以避免非局部效应和流形混合带来的偏差，但小邻域内数据有限会导致高估计方差。需要一种方差减少策略来改善LID估计的稳定性。

Method: 提出基于子袋装（subbagging）的集成方法，通过保留最近邻距离的局部分布来减少方差。分析了采样率、k-NN大小和集成规模三个超参数的相互作用，并探索了将袋装法与邻域平滑相结合的不同方式。

Result: 在超参数空间的广泛区域内，袋装估计器相比非袋装基线能显著降低方差和均方误差，同时对偏差有可控影响。结合袋装与邻域平滑能进一步显著提升LID估计性能。

Conclusion: 子袋装集成方法能有效降低LID估计的方差，通过合理选择采样率、k-NN大小和集成规模等超参数，可以在保持局部性的同时改善估计稳定性，为实际应用提供了可指导的超参数选择框架。

Abstract: The theory of Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID) has become a valuable tool for characterizing local complexity within and across data manifolds, supporting a range of data mining and machine learning tasks. Accurate LID estimation requires samples drawn from small neighborhoods around each query to avoid biases from nonlocal effects and potential manifold mixing, yet limited data within such neighborhoods tends to cause high estimation variance. As a variance reduction strategy, we propose an ensemble approach that uses subbagging to preserve the local distribution of nearest neighbor (NN) distances. The main challenge is that the uniform reduction in total sample size within each subsample increases the proximity threshold for finding a fixed number k of NNs around the query. As a result, in the specific context of LID estimation, the sampling rate has an additional, complex interplay with the neighborhood size, where both combined determine the sample size as well as the locality and resolution considered for estimation. We analyze both theoretically and experimentally how the choice of the sampling rate and the k-NN size used for LID estimation, alongside the ensemble size, affects performance, enabling informed prior selection of these hyper-parameters depending on application-based preferences. Our results indicate that within broad and well-characterized regions of the hyper-parameters space, using a bagged estimator will most often significantly reduce variance as well as the mean squared error when compared to the corresponding non-bagged baseline, with controllable impact on bias. We additionally propose and evaluate different ways of combining bagging with neighborhood smoothing for substantial further improvements on LID estimation performance.

</details>


### [130] [Marchuk: Efficient Global Weather Forecasting from Mid-Range to Sub-Seasonal Scales via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24428)
*Arsen Kuzhamuratov,Mikhail Zhirnov,Andrey Kuznetsov,Ivan Oseledets,Konstantin Sobolev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Marchuk是一个用于全球天气预报的生成式潜在流匹配模型，覆盖中程到次季节时间尺度，预测范围可达30天，具有高计算效率和强预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的次季节天气预报是一个重大挑战，因为大气固有的混沌特性限制了传统模型在中程（约15天）之外的预测能力。

Method: Marchuk是一个生成式潜在流匹配模型，以当前天气图为条件，在学习的潜在空间中自回归预测后续天气图。它用可训练位置嵌入替换旋转位置编码，并扩展时间上下文窗口，以增强模型在潜在预测中表示和传播长程时间依赖的能力。

Result: 尽管只有2.76亿参数的紧凑架构，Marchuk的性能与拥有16亿参数的更大模型LaDCast相当，同时推理速度显著更高。

Conclusion: Marchuk为次季节天气预报提供了一种计算高效且性能强大的解决方案，其代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Accurate subseasonal weather forecasting remains a major challenge due to the inherently chaotic nature of the atmosphere, which limits the predictive skill of conventional models beyond the mid-range horizon (approximately 15 days). In this work, we present \textit{Marchuk}, a generative latent flow-matching model for global weather forecasting spanning mid-range to subseasonal timescales, with prediction horizons of up to 30 days. Marchuk conditions on current-day weather maps and autoregressively predicts subsequent days' weather maps within the learned latent space. We replace rotary positional encodings (RoPE) with trainable positional embeddings and extend the temporal context window, which together enhance the model's ability to represent and propagate long-range temporal dependencies during latent forecasting. Marchuk offers two key advantages: high computational efficiency and strong predictive performance. Despite its compact architecture of only 276 million parameters, the model achieves performance comparable to LaDCast, a substantially larger model with 1.6 billion parameters, while operating at significantly higher inference speeds. We open-source our inference code and model at: https://v-gen-ai.github.io/Marchuk/

</details>


### [131] [Conformalized Transfer Learning for Li-ion Battery State of Health Forecasting under Manufacturing and Usage Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24475)
*Samuel Filgueira da Silva,Mehmet Fatih Ozkan,Faissal El Idrissi,Marcello Canova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知的迁移学习框架，结合LSTM、MMD域适应和CP不确定性量化，用于锂离子电池健康状态预测，解决实验室数据到实际应用场景的泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特定实验室条件校准的健康状态预测模型难以泛化到存在制造差异或不同运行条件的新电池，需要解决领域偏移问题并提高预测的可信度。

Method: 采用LSTM模型，结合最大均值差异进行域适应以对齐模拟域和目标域的潜在特征分布，并使用共形预测进行不确定性量化，提供校准的预测区间。

Result: 该框架提高了健康状态预测在异质电池间的泛化能力和可信度，通过虚拟电池数据集捕捉制造和运行条件的真实变异性。

Conclusion: 提出的不确定性感知迁移学习框架能够有效解决电池健康状态预测中的领域偏移问题，同时提供可靠的预测不确定性量化，增强了模型的实用性和可信度。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of state-of-health (SOH) is essential for ensuring safe and reliable operation of lithium-ion cells. However, existing models calibrated on laboratory tests at specific conditions often fail to generalize to new cells that differ due to small manufacturing variations or operate under different conditions. To address this challenge, an uncertainty-aware transfer learning framework is proposed, combining a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) model with domain adaptation via Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) and uncertainty quantification through Conformal Prediction (CP). The LSTM model is trained on a virtual battery dataset designed to capture real-world variability in electrode manufacturing and operating conditions. MMD aligns latent feature distributions between simulated and target domains to mitigate domain shift, while CP provides calibrated, distribution-free prediction intervals. This framework improves both the generalization and trustworthiness of SOH forecasts across heterogeneous cells.

</details>


### [132] [Uniform Laws of Large Numbers in Product Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24493)
*Ron Holzman,Shay Moran,Alexander Shlimovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了笛卡尔乘积空间中的一致大数定律，在分布相对于其边际乘积绝对连续的条件下，证明了事件族的一致大数定律成立当且仅当其线性VC维有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Vapnik-Chervonenkis理论中，一致大数定律由VC维的有限性刻画。本研究旨在探索笛卡尔乘积空间中的一致收敛现象，特别是在与乘积结构兼容的分布假设下，研究更精细的收敛条件。

Method: 假设分布相对于其边际乘积绝对连续，引入线性VC维概念（定义为位于轴平行线上的最大可粉碎集大小），构建了与传统经验均值估计器显著不同的新估计器，并证明这种偏离在设定中是不可避免的。

Result: 在给定分布假设下，事件族的一致大数定律成立当且仅当其线性VC维有限。线性VC维始终不超过经典VC维，但可以任意小（例如，R^d中凸集族的线性VC维为2，而经典VC维在d≥2时为无穷大）。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了VC理论到乘积空间，建立了线性VC维作为一致收敛的新特征量，揭示了在乘积结构下需要不同于传统经验均值估计器的估计方法，并提出了关于定量样本复杂度界的开放问题。

Abstract: Uniform laws of large numbers form a cornerstone of Vapnik--Chervonenkis theory, where they are characterized by the finiteness of the VC dimension. In this work, we study uniform convergence phenomena in cartesian product spaces, under assumptions on the underlying distribution that are compatible with the product structure. Specifically, we assume that the distribution is absolutely continuous with respect to the product of its marginals, a condition that captures many natural settings, including product distributions, sparse mixtures of product distributions, distributions with low mutual information, and more.
  We show that, under this assumption, a uniform law of large numbers holds for a family of events if and only if the linear VC dimension of the family is finite. The linear VC dimension is defined as the maximum size of a shattered set that lies on an axis-parallel line, namely, a set of vectors that agree on all but at most one coordinate. This dimension is always at most the classical VC dimension, yet it can be arbitrarily smaller. For instance, the family of convex sets in $\mathbb{R}^d$ has linear VC dimension $2$, while its VC dimension is infinite already for $d\ge 2$. Our proofs rely on estimator that departs substantially from the standard empirical mean estimator and exhibits more intricate structure. We show that such deviations from the standard empirical mean estimator are unavoidable in this setting. Throughout the paper, we propose several open questions, with a particular focus on quantitative sample complexity bounds.

</details>


### [133] [Project and Generate: Divergence-Free Neural Operators for Incompressible Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24500)
*Xigui Li,Hongwei Zhang,Ruoxi Jiang,Deshu Chen,Chensen Lin,Limei Han,Yuan Qi,Xin Guo,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，将不可压缩连续性方程作为硬约束应用于确定性和生成式流体建模，通过谱Leray投影和旋度前推确保物理一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的流体动力学模型通常在无约束函数空间中运行，导致物理不可接受、不稳定的模拟。基于惩罚的方法提供软正则化但无结构保证，会产生虚假发散和长期崩溃。

Method: 1) 对确定性模型：集成基于Helmholtz-Hodge分解的可微分谱Leray投影，将回归假设空间限制到物理可接受的散度自由速度场；2) 对生成模型：当先验不兼容时，通过基于旋度的前推构建散度自由高斯参考测度，确保整个概率流保持子空间一致性。

Result: 在2D Navier-Stokes方程上的实验表明，该方法实现了精确的不可压缩性（达到离散化误差水平），并显著改善了稳定性和物理一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将不可压缩性作为硬约束而非软正则化，为确定性和生成式流体建模提供了结构保证，解决了传统方法中物理不可接受和长期不稳定问题。

Abstract: Learning-based models for fluid dynamics often operate in unconstrained function spaces, leading to physically inadmissible, unstable simulations. While penalty-based methods offer soft regularization, they provide no structural guarantees, resulting in spurious divergence and long-term collapse. In this work, we introduce a unified framework that enforces the incompressible continuity equation as a hard, intrinsic constraint for both deterministic and generative modeling. First, to project deterministic models onto the divergence-free subspace, we integrate a differentiable spectral Leray projection grounded in the Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition, which restricts the regression hypothesis space to physically admissible velocity fields. Second, to generate physically consistent distributions, we show that simply projecting model outputs is insufficient when the prior is incompatible. To address this, we construct a divergence-free Gaussian reference measure via a curl-based pushforward, ensuring the entire probability flow remains subspace-consistent by construction. Experiments on 2D Navier-Stokes equations demonstrate exact incompressibility up to discretization error and substantially improved stability and physical consistency.

</details>


### [134] [Towards Safe Learning-Based Non-Linear Model Predictive Control through Recurrent Neural Network Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24503)
*Mihaela-Larisa Clement,Mónika Farsang,Agnes Poks,Johannes Edelmann,Manfred Plöchl,Radu Grosu,Ezio Bartocci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sequential-AMPC方法，通过跨预测时域共享参数的序列神经策略生成MPC候选控制序列，结合安全增强在线评估和回退机制，减少专家数据需求并提高闭环安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)在实际部署中受限于在线计算复杂度，尤其是在嵌入式硬件上求解非线性规划问题代价高昂。基于学习的NMPC近似方法虽然将计算转移到离线，但通常需要大量专家数据集和昂贵的训练成本。

Method: 提出Sequential-AMPC序列神经策略，通过跨预测时域共享参数生成MPC候选控制序列。部署时，将策略包装在安全增强的在线评估和回退机制中，形成Safe Sequential-AMPC。

Result: 相比前馈策略基线，Sequential-AMPC需要更少的专家MPC轨迹，产生具有更高可行率和改进闭环安全性的候选序列。在高维系统上表现出更好的学习动态和更少epoch的性能提升，同时保持稳定的验证改进，而前馈基线可能停滞。

Conclusion: Sequential-AMPC提供了一种有效的NMPC近似方法，显著减少了专家数据需求，提高了控制序列的可行性和闭环安全性，特别适用于高维复杂系统。

Abstract: The practical deployment of nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) is often limited by online computation: solving a nonlinear program at high control rates can be expensive on embedded hardware, especially when models are complex or horizons are long. Learning-based NMPC approximations shift this computation offline but typically demand large expert datasets and costly training. We propose Sequential-AMPC, a sequential neural policy that generates MPC candidate control sequences by sharing parameters across the prediction horizon. For deployment, we wrap the policy in a safety-augmented online evaluation and fallback mechanism, yielding Safe Sequential-AMPC. Compared to a naive feedforward policy baseline across several benchmarks, Sequential-AMPC requires substantially fewer expert MPC rollouts and yields candidate sequences with higher feasibility rates and improved closed-loop safety. On high-dimensional systems, it also exhibits better learning dynamics and performance in fewer epochs while maintaining stable validation improvement where the feedforward baseline can stagnate.

</details>


### [135] [TuneShift-KD: Knowledge Distillation and Transfer for Fine-tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24518)
*Yushi Guan,Jeanine Ohene-Agyei,Daniel Kwan,Jean Sebastien Dandurand,Yifei Zhang,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TuneShift-KD：一种无需原始训练数据、仅需少量代表性提示即可从微调模型中自动蒸馏专业知识的创新方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着新LLM架构和预训练模型不断涌现，将专业领域知识从已微调的模型迁移到新模型变得日益重要。然而，原始专业数据常因隐私或商业限制而无法获取，需要从微调模型中蒸馏知识到不同的预训练模型。

Method: 基于困惑度差异识别专业知识：当微调模型响应自信（低困惑度）而基础模型困难（高困惑度）时，表明查询对应微调模型学习的专业知识。利用这一洞察创建合成训练数据集，通过迭代过程生成更多类似提示，无需训练判别器或访问原始数据集。

Result: 实验表明，使用TuneShift-KD微调的模型比先前方法获得更高准确率，实现了更有效的专业知识迁移和更便捷的部署。

Conclusion: TuneShift-KD提供了一种自动化、数据高效的方法，仅需微调模型、基础模型和少量代表性提示即可实现专业知识的有效迁移，解决了数据不可用场景下的知识蒸馏挑战。

Abstract: To embed domain-specific or specialized knowledge into pre-trained foundation models, fine-tuning using techniques such as parameter efficient fine-tuning (e.g. LoRA) is a common practice. However, as new LLM architectures and pre-trained models emerge, transferring this specialized knowledge to newer models becomes an important task. In many scenarios, the original specialized data may be unavailable due to privacy or commercial restrictions, necessitating distillation and transfer of this specialized knowledge from the fine-tuned base model to a different pre-trained model. We present TuneShift-KD, a novel approach that automatically distills specialized knowledge from a fine-tuned model to a target model using only a few examples representative of the specialized information. Our key insight is that specialized knowledge can be identified through perplexity differences between base and fine-tuned models: prompts where the fine-tuned model responds confidently (low perplexity), but the base model struggles (high perplexity), indicate queries corresponding to the specialized knowledge learned by the fine-tuned model. TuneShift-KD leverages this insight to create a synthetic training dataset to transfer the specialized knowledge. Using an iterative process, TuneShift-KD generates more prompts similar to those that generated responses with specialized knowledge. TuneShift-KD does not require training discriminators or access to training datasets. It is an automated approach that only requires the initial fine-tuned and base models and a few representative prompts. Our experiments demonstrate that models fine-tuned using TuneShift-KD achieve higher accuracy than prior approaches, enabling ease of deployment and more effective transfer of the specialized knowledge.

</details>


### [136] [Scaling Recurrence-aware Foundation Models for Clinical Records via Next-Visit Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24562)
*Haresh Rengaraj Rajamohan,Xiang Gao,Weicheng Zhu,Shih-Lun Huang,Long Chen,Gabe Schulman,Huizhen Jin,Shengduo Li,Yixuan Wang,Huidi Yang,Kyunghyun Cho,Cem M. Deniz,Narges Razavian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAVEN是一种针对电子健康记录的新型生成式预训练策略，通过递归感知的下次就诊事件预测来学习生成临床事件，解决了重复事件对评估指标的干扰问题，并在数据受限的计算饱和状态下研究了扩展行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模预训练在语言建模中取得了革命性进展，但其在结构化电子健康记录领域的潜力尚未充分探索。当前EHR基础模型评估存在一个关键缺陷：重复事件标记会混淆性能指标，因为无法区分新发病例和后续发生。

Method: 提出RAVEN（递归感知下次就诊事件预测）生成式预训练策略，基于超过100万独特个体的数据集，模型学习自回归生成下次就诊的标记化临床事件。引入重复事件预测的正则化，并在数据受限、计算饱和状态下实证研究扩展行为。

Result: 在零样本预测多种疾病发病率方面，RAVEN可与完全微调的基于表示的Transformer模型相媲美，并优于广泛使用的基于模拟的下次标记方法。无需额外参数更新，模型能够在有损临床代码映射和特征覆盖差距的情况下泛化到外部患者队列。

Conclusion: RAVEN展示了在EHR数据上进行生成式预训练的有效性，强调了区分新发事件和重复事件的重要性，并指出在数据受限情况下单纯增加模型规模而不相应增加数据量是次优的。模型展示了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: While large-scale pretraining has revolutionized language modeling, its potential remains underexplored in healthcare with structured electronic health records (EHRs). We present RAVEN, a novel generative pretraining strategy for sequential EHR data based on Recurrence-Aware next-Visit EveNt prediction. Leveraging a dataset of over one million unique individuals, our model learns to autoregressively generate tokenized clinical events for the next visit conditioned on patient history. We introduce regularization on predicting repeated events and highlight a key pitfall in EHR-based foundation model evaluations: repeated event tokens can inflate performance metrics when new onsets are not distinguished from subsequent occurrences. Furthermore, we empirically investigate the scaling behaviors in a data-constrained, compute-saturated regime, showing that simply increasing model size is suboptimal without commensurate increases in data volume. We evaluate our model via zero-shot prediction for forecasting the incidence of a diverse set of diseases, where it rivals fully fine-tuned representation-based Transformer models and outperforms widely used simulation-based next-token approaches. Finally, without additional parameter updates, we show that RAVEN can generalize to an external patient cohort under lossy clinical code mappings and feature coverage gaps.

</details>


### [137] [DreamerAD: Efficient Reinforcement Learning via Latent World Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24587)
*Pengxuan Yang,Yupeng Zheng,Deheng Qian,Zebin Xing,Qichao Zhang,Linbo Wang,Yichen Zhang,Shaoyu Guo,Zhongpu Xia,Qiang Chen,Junyu Han,Lingyun Xu,Yifeng Pan,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DreamerAD：首个基于潜在世界模型的自动驾驶强化学习框架，通过将扩散采样从100步压缩到1步实现80倍加速，在NavSim v2上达到87.7 EPDMS的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界驾驶数据上训练RL策略成本高昂且存在安全风险。现有基于像素级扩散的世界模型虽然支持安全想象训练，但多步扩散推理延迟（2秒/帧）阻碍了高频RL交互。

Method: 利用视频生成模型的去噪潜在特征，通过三种关键机制：1）通过递归多分辨率步压缩降低采样复杂度的捷径强制；2）直接在潜在表示上操作的自回归密集奖励模型；3）用于GRPO的高斯词汇采样，将探索约束在物理可行轨迹内。

Result: 在NavSim v2上达到87.7 EPDMS，建立最先进性能，同时实现80倍加速（从100步压缩到1步），保持视觉可解释性。

Conclusion: DreamerAD证明了潜在空间强化学习在自动驾驶中的有效性，通过高效的世界模型压缩实现了安全、低成本的策略训练。

Abstract: We introduce DreamerAD, the first latent world model framework that enables efficient reinforcement learning for autonomous driving by compressing diffusion sampling from 100 steps to 1 - achieving 80x speedup while maintaining visual interpretability. Training RL policies on real-world driving data incurs prohibitive costs and safety risks. While existing pixel-level diffusion world models enable safe imagination-based training, they suffer from multi-step diffusion inference latency (2s/frame) that prevents high-frequency RL interaction. Our approach leverages denoised latent features from video generation models through three key mechanisms: (1) shortcut forcing that reduces sampling complexity via recursive multi-resolution step compression, (2) an autoregressive dense reward model operating directly on latent representations for fine-grained credit assignment, and (3) Gaussian vocabulary sampling for GRPO that constrains exploration to physically plausible trajectories. DreamerAD achieves 87.7 EPDMS on NavSim v2, establishing state-of-the-art performance and demonstrating that latent-space RL is effective for autonomous driving.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Effective rates for continuous-time quasi-Fejér monotone dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23708)
*Anton Freund,Nicholas Pischke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为满足拟Fejér单调性连续时间模拟的度量空间中的动力系统提供了定量收敛结果，包括紧度量空间中的强收敛结果（配备连续时间亚稳定性速率）和非紧空间中的扩展结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究满足拟Fejér单调性连续时间模拟的动力系统的定量收敛理论，为许多动力系统和微分方程/包含提供统一的定量分析框架。

Method: 采用连续时间拟Fejér单调性作为统一性质，为紧度量空间中的动力系统构造显式有效的连续时间亚稳定性速率，并扩展到非紧空间（需正则性假设）。

Result: 获得了紧度量空间中动力系统的强收敛结果（配备显式构造的连续时间亚稳定性速率），并将结果扩展到非紧空间（获得类似均匀的收敛速率），这些结果在现有一般性下具有新颖性。

Conclusion: 连续时间拟Fejér单调性可作为许多动力系统的统一性质，为希尔伯特空间中的经典一阶/二阶动力系统、非线性Hadamard空间中的（广义）梯度流及相关半群提供全面的定量理论。

Abstract: We provide quantitative convergence results for continuous-time dynamical systems in metric spaces that satisfy a continuous-time analog of quasi-Fejér monotonicity. More precisely, we provide a (strong) convergence result for such dynamical systems over compact metric spaces which is quantitatively outfitted with a continuous-time rate of metastability, which moreover can be explicitly and effectively constructed in a very uniform way, only depending on a few moduli representing quantitative witnesses to key properties of the dynamical system and a measure for the compactness of the space. We further show how this convergence result can be extended to non-compact spaces under a regularity assumption of the associated problem, where moreover rates of convergence can then be explicitly constructed which are similarly uniform. In both cases, already the associated ``infinitary'' convergence result is qualitatively novel in its present generality. Beyond this abstract quantitative theory for such dynamical systems, we motivate how the presently studied continuous-time variant of quasi-Fejér monotonicity naturally occurs as a unifying property of many dynamical systems and differential equations and inclusions, and in that way can be used to provide a comprehensive quantitative theory for many such dynamical systems. We illustrate this with three case studies for both classical first- and second-order dynamical systems in Hilbert spaces as well as (generalized) gradient flows and associated semigroups in nonlinear Hadamard spaces.

</details>


### [139] [Risk-Aware Linear-Quadratic Regulation with Temporally Coupled States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23737)
*Chuanning Wei,Kin Fung Li,Dionysis Kalogerias,Margaret P. Chapman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限时域离散时间线性二次调节（LQR）问题的新框架，该框架同时惩罚状态轨迹的时间变异性和随机变异性，使用户能够在状态调节与减少时间变异性之间取得平衡，并具有明确的风险敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LQR问题主要关注状态调节，但实际应用中状态轨迹的时间变异性和随机变异性同样重要。现有方法缺乏同时处理这两种变异性的统一框架，特别是在具有时间耦合状态的场景中。

Method: 将预测方差这一风险度量扩展到具有时间耦合状态的设置中，构建了一个新的LQR问题框架，该框架在代价函数中同时包含状态调节项、时间变异性惩罚项和随机变异性惩罚项。

Result: 数值实验展示了时间耦合在风险感知和风险中性控制设置中的影响。特别观察到，仅明确惩罚时间变异性也能有效减少随机变异性，表明两种变异性之间存在内在联系。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为平衡状态调节、时间变异性和随机变异性提供了系统方法，扩展了传统LQR问题的适用范围，在风险敏感控制中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We formulate and solve a discrete-time linear-quadratic regulation (LQR) problem in a finite horizon that penalizes temporal variability and stochastic variability of the state trajectory. Our approach enables the user to strike a balance between regulating the state and reducing temporal variability, with explicit sensitivity to risk. We achieve this by extending a risk measure called predictive variance to a setting with temporally coupled states. Numerical examples demonstrate the effect of temporal coupling in both risk-aware and risk-neutral control settings. Particularly, we observe that explicitly penalizing temporal variability alone can also reduce stochastic variability.

</details>


### [140] [Utility-Invariant Support Selection and Eventwise Decoupling for Simultaneous Independent Multi-Outcome Bets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24064)
*Christopher D. Long*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文证明了在有限结果同时独立事件下，具有非负赌注和内生现金头寸的期望效用最大化问题中，最优投资组合的活跃支撑集具有短支撑特性，且该支撑集与效用函数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究同时独立事件下的期望效用最大化问题，探索最优投资组合支撑集的性质，特别是支撑集与效用函数的独立性关系。

Method: 通过分析一阶条件，推导出预算乘数λ与活跃概率质量P_ℓ,A、活跃价格质量Q_ℓ,A之间的精确恒等式，并利用状态价格几何学方法证明支撑集特性。

Result: 证明了短支撑定理：在固定支撑族和具有正现金的最优投资组合下，活跃支撑集是各事件单事件支撑集的并集，且该支撑集独立于效用函数。

Conclusion: 同时独立事件下的效用不变支撑定理可以从状态价格几何学推导得出，关键在于正确识别延续因子K_ℓ^(U)，这扩展了Smoczynski和Miles在自由暴露同注分彩设定中的结果。

Abstract: For simultaneous independent events with finitely many outcomes, consider the expected-utility problem with nonnegative wagers and an endogenous cash position. We prove a short support theorem for a broad class of strictly increasing strictly concave utilities. On any fixed support family and at any optimal portfolio with positive cash, summing the active first-order conditions and comparing that sum with cash stationarity yields the exact identity \[ \fracλ{K_{\ell}^{(U)}}=\frac{1-P_{\ell,A}}{1-Q_{\ell,A}}, \] where $P_{\ell,A}$ and $Q_{\ell,A}$ are the active probability and price masses of event $\ell$, $λ$ is the budget multiplier, and $K_{\ell}^{(U)}$ is the continuation factor seen by inactive outcomes of that event. Consequently, after sorting each event by the edge ratio $p_{\ell i}/π_{\ell i}$, the exact active support is the eventwise union of the single-event supports, and this support is independent of the utility function. The single-event utility-invariant support theorem is already explicit in the free-exposure pari-mutuel setting in Smoczynski and Miles; the point of the present note is that the simultaneous independent-events analogue follows from the same state-price geometry once the right continuation factor is identified.

</details>


### [141] [Theoretical properties of the eigenvector method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24274)
*Sándor Bozóki,László Csató*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文讨论了右特征向量作为成对比较矩阵权重推导方法的五个潜在缺陷：右左不对称性、群体选择不一致性、偏好强度依赖性、权重反直觉变化以及帕累托非效率性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管右特征向量是成对比较矩阵权重推导的经典方法，但文献已识别出该方法存在一些潜在弱点。本文旨在系统讨论这些理论缺陷，通过具体示例突出这些问题，并为未来研究提供方向。

Method: 采用理论分析和反例构造的方法，通过具体的成对比较矩阵示例来展示右特征向量方法的五个理论缺陷。每个问题都通过数学证明和数值示例进行说明。

Result: 论文明确展示了右特征向量方法的五个具体缺陷：1）右左特征向量不对称性；2）群体决策中的选择不一致性；3）权重排序对偏好强度的依赖性；4）权重值的反直觉变化；5）帕累托非效率性。所有缺陷都通过具体示例得到验证。

Conclusion: 右特征向量作为成对比较矩阵的权重推导方法存在多个理论缺陷，这些缺陷在群体决策和实际应用中可能产生问题。论文提出了若干开放性问题，为未来研究提供了方向，建议在特定应用场景中谨慎使用该方法或考虑替代方案。

Abstract: A classical proposal to derive weights from a pairwise comparison matrix is the right eigenvector. The literature has identified some potential weaknesses of this method in previous decades. This chapter discusses five of these issues. First, right-left asymmetry emerges because of the difference between the right and inverse left eigenvectors. Second, group incoherence for choice means that, in group decision-making problems, the ranking given by the aggregated individual weight vectors is not guaranteed to coincide with the ranking derived from the aggregated pairwise comparison matrix. Third, the ranking based on the right eigenvector may depend on the intensity of the preferences, represented by taking a positive power of all comparisons. Fourth, both the ranking position and the normalised weight of an object might change counter-intuitively after modifying a particular comparison. Fifth, the right eigenvector is not necessarily Pareto efficient: a dominating weight vector that approximates each pairwise comparison at least as well, with an improvement in at least one position, could exist. All violations of the theoretical properties are highlighted by illustrative examples. We also present several open questions in order to inspire future research.

</details>


### [142] [The Riemannian Landing Method: From projected gradient flows to SQP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24309)
*Florentin Goyens,Florian Feppon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的几何框架，将着陆方法（landing methods）与经典优化方法（如投影梯度流、零空间梯度流、SQP和增广拉格朗日法）联系起来，并基于斜投影算子的度量参数化分析了着陆方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 着陆方法在黎曼矩阵优化中作为处理非线性等式约束的有效方案而出现，它避免了昂贵的回缩操作。然而，需要建立一个统一的几何框架来揭示着陆方法与经典优化方法之间的联系，并系统化地设计度量以获得显式搜索方向。

Method: 1. 提出统一的几何框架，展示在合适的黎曼度量选择下，着陆算法包含投影梯度流、零空间梯度流、SQP和某种形式的增广拉格朗日法；2. 通过斜投影算子场的度量参数化分析着陆方法，阐明正交性、切向和法向度量的作用；3. 提出使用自适应步长的全局收敛着陆方法，采用基于价值函数的Armijo条件的回溯线搜索。

Result: 1. 证明了二次收敛的着陆方法本质上重现了二次收敛的SQP方法；2. 建立了着陆方法与经典优化方法的统一联系；3. 为矩阵优化问题提供了系统化的度量设计指南，能够获得闭式搜索方向；4. 提出的自适应步长方法不需要先验的Lipschitz常数知识。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一的几何框架揭示了着陆方法与经典优化方法之间的深刻联系，为黎曼矩阵优化提供了理论基础。基于斜投影算子的度量参数化框架不仅恢复了正交约束问题中的现有构造，还为设计新度量以获得显式搜索方向提供了系统化指导。自适应步长方法确保了全局收敛性。

Abstract: Landing methods have recently emerged in Riemannian matrix optimization as efficient schemes for handling nonlinear equality constraints without resorting to costly retractions. These methods decompose the search direction into tangent and normal components, enabling asymptotic feasibility while maintaining inexpensive updates. In this work, we provide a unifying geometric framework which reveals that, under suitable choices of Riemannian metric, the landing algorithm encompasses several classical optimization methods such as projected and null-space gradient flows, Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP), and a certain form of the augmented Lagrangian method. In particular, we show that a quadratically convergent landing method essentially reproduces the quadratically convergent SQP method. These connections also allow us to propose a globally convergent landing method using adaptive step sizes. The backtracking line search satisfies an Armijo condition on a merit function, and does not require prior knowledge of Lipschitz constants.
  Our second key contribution is to analyze landing methods through a geometric parameterization of the metric in terms of fields of oblique projectors and associated metric restrictions. This viewpoint disentangles the roles of orthogonality, tangent and normal metrics, and elucidates how to design the metric to obtain explicit tangent and normal updates. For matrix optimization, this framework not only recovers recent constructions in the literature for problems with orthogonality constraints, but also provides systematic guidelines for designing new metrics that admit closed-form search directions.

</details>


### [143] [A unified modeling framework and improved formulations for single-hoist cyclic scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24316)
*Markó Horváth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了循环吊车调度问题的建模，通过统一视角分析现有MIP模型的不一致性，提出CP基准模型和改进的MIP公式，并建立了包含基准实例和实现的公开库以支持可重复研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电镀生产线中的循环吊车调度问题已有数十年研究，但文献中存在一个令人困惑的现象：不同研究对相同基准实例报告不同的最优周期时间，这限制了计算结果的比较性和可重复性。

Method: 从统一视角重新建模循环吊车调度问题，引入一致的建模方法分析现有混合整数线性规划（MIP）公式，识别建模不一致性，提出约束规划（CP）基准模型并推导改进的MIP公式。

Result: 通过广泛的计算实验比较了所研究公式的强度和性能，并建立了一个公开可用的库，包含基准实例和多个CP及MIP公式的实现。

Conclusion: 本文澄清了现有建模方法之间的关系，提供了基准CP模型和改进的MIP公式，并通过公开库支持循环吊车调度问题的可重复研究，解决了文献中结果不一致的问题。

Abstract: The cyclic hoist scheduling problem originates in electroplating lines, where a single or multiple hoists transport parts between processing tanks subject to technological constraints. The objective is typically to determine a cyclic sequence of hoist movements that minimizes the cycle time while satisfying travel and processing constraints. Although the problem has been widely studied for several decades, the literature contains a puzzling phenomenon: different studies often report different optimal cycle times for the same benchmark instances, which limits the comparability and reproducibility of computational results.
  In this paper, we revisit the modeling of cyclic hoist scheduling problems from a unified perspective. We introduce a consistent modeling approach for single-hoist problems and analyze several mixed-integer linear programming (MIP) formulations proposed in the literature. Our analysis identifies modeling inconsistencies and clarifies the relationships between existing formulations. Based on these observations, we propose straightforward constraint programming (CP) models that can serve as baseline approaches, and we also derive improved MIP~formulations. Extensive computational experiments compare the strength and performance of the investigated formulations.
  To support reproducible research, we also provide a publicly available library containing benchmark instances and implementations of several CP and MIP~formulations for single-hoist cyclic hoist scheduling.

</details>


### [144] [Optimal local interventions in the two-dimensional Abelian sandpile model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24459)
*Maike C. de Jongh,Richard J. Boucherie,M. N. M. van Lieshout*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究阿贝尔沙堆模型中减少雪崩规模的干预策略，通过移除选定位置的沙粒来降低雪崩大小，首次对干预效果进行严格分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿贝尔沙堆模型是自组织临界性的典型例子，其雪崩现象可类比地震、森林火灾等灾害。研究如何通过干预减少雪崩规模，为灾害防控提供理论参考。

Method: 开发并形式化扩展方法计算临界顶点连通分量引发的期望雪崩大小；分析正方形分量中干预效果；研究最优干预位置选择策略。

Result: 最优干预位置需要在减少最大雪崩规模与增加缓解雪崩数量之间取得平衡；对正方形分量中的干预效果进行了显式分析。

Conclusion: 首次对阿贝尔沙堆模型中的干预效果进行严格分析，揭示了最优干预策略的平衡特性，为理解复杂系统中的灾害缓解提供理论框架。

Abstract: The Abelian sandpile model serves as a canonical example of self-organized criticality. This critical behavior manifests itself through large cascading events triggered by small perturbations. Such large-scale events, known as avalanches, are often regarded as stylized representations of catastrophic phenomena, such as earthquakes or forest fires. Motivated by this perspective, we study strategies to reduce avalanche sizes. We provide a first rigorous analysis of the impact of interventions in the Abelian sandpile model, considering a setting in which an external controller can perturb a configuration by removing sand grains at selected locations. We first develop and formalize an extended method to compute the expected size of an avalanche originating from a connected component of critical vertices, i.e., vertices at maximum height. Using this method, we characterize the structure of avalanches starting from square components and explicitly analyze the effect of interventions in such components. Our results show that the optimal intervention locations strike an interesting balance between reduction of largest avalanche sizes and increasing the number of mitigated avalanches.

</details>


### [145] [Model Predictive Path Integral Control as Preconditioned Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24489)
*Mahyar Fazlyab,Sina Sharifi,Jiarui Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: MPPI控制是一种基于采样的轨迹优化方法，本文通过变分优化理论视角重新解释MPPI，将其视为带KL正则化的分布优化问题，并推导出收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI控制虽然在实际应用中很流行，但其优化结构尚未被完全理解。本文旨在从优化理论角度深入理解MPPI，建立其变分解释框架，从而能够进行收敛性分析。

Method: 将约束轨迹优化问题提升为带KL正则化的分布优化问题，简化为负对数配分函数（自由能）目标。对于一般参数族，得到分布参数的预条件梯度方法；对于固定协方差高斯族，证明经典MPPI恰好是步长为1的预条件梯度下降步。

Result: 建立了MPPI的变分优化理论解释，推导出显式的光滑常数上界，给出了保证精确MPPI下降的充分条件。数值实验验证了理论，并展示了关键超参数对性能的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为MPPI控制提供了坚实的优化理论基础，将其解释为带KL正则化的分布优化问题的预条件梯度方法，从而能够进行收敛性分析，为超参数选择和算法改进提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is a popular sampling-based method for trajectory optimization in nonlinear and nonconvex settings, yet its optimization structure remains only partially understood. We develop a variational, optimization-theoretic interpretation of MPPI by lifting constrained trajectory optimization to a KL-regularized problem over distributions and reducing it to a negative log-partition (free-energy) objective over a tractable sampling family. For a general parametric family, this yields a preconditioned gradient method on the distribution parameters and a natural multi-step extension of MPPI. For the fixed-covariance Gaussian family, we show that classical MPPI is recovered exactly as a preconditioned gradient descent step with unit step size. This interpretation enables a direct convergence analysis: under bounded feasible sets, we derive an explicit upper bound on the smoothness constant and a simple sufficient condition guaranteeing descent of exact MPPI. Numerical experiments support the theory and illustrate the effect of key hyperparameters on performance.

</details>


### [146] [Optimal control of infinite-dimensional dissipative systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24507)
*Anthony Hastir,Timo Reis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将无限维耗散系统的线性二次最优控制问题（可能具有不定代价函数）转化为具有非负代价函数的等价问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限维耗散系统在线性二次最优控制框架下的问题，特别是当代价函数可能为不定（非正定）时的理论处理

Method: 假设存在存储函数，证明不定最优控制问题等价于具有非负代价函数的线性二次最优控制问题，建立相应值函数之间的关系，并推导相关的算子Lur'e方程

Result: 成功建立了不定代价函数问题与非负代价函数问题之间的等价性，给出了值函数关系的理论结果，并通过多个示例验证了理论的有效性

Conclusion: 对于无限维耗散系统，通过存储函数的存在性假设，可以将具有不定代价函数的线性二次最优控制问题转化为标准的非负代价函数问题，为这类问题的理论分析和求解提供了新途径

Abstract: We study the linear-quadratic optimal control problem for infinite-dimensional dissipative systems with possibly indefinite cost functional. Under the assumption that a storage function exists, we show that this indefinite optimal control problem is equivalent to a linear-quadratic optimal control problem with a nonnegative cost functional. We establish the relationship between the corresponding value functions and present the associated operator Lur'e equation. Finally, we illustrate our results with several examples.

</details>
